- ST-LLM-008 (gap): only the finance role with the right on the whole tenant runs and reads the reports; a report of a currency other than the one of the attempts is refused (422 `CURRENCY_MISMATCH`) instead of being converted - the conversion of a foreign invoice belongs with the exchange rates of FIN.
- ST-LLM-008 (decision, screen): LLM-21 (`apps/web/src/screens/llm/routelog/`) is two parts gated apart - the routing log (`llm.audit.read`, metadata only, never content) and the reconciliations (`llm.finops`); the variance detail is built from the list row since there is no read by id; money is shown with a BigInt grouping helper that keeps the eight places and the sign (the direction of the variance is read from the sign of the string); the form keeps its values after a run because the documented flow is to run again when the late usage arrives, with a new idempotency key.
- ST-LLM-008 (gap, screen): the decision of the owner of a budget (`post_budgets_id_reconcile`) has no control on LLM-21 - the reports carry no budget id, budget version or reservation ids, so it needs a read that links a variance to its budget and its waiting reservations; the columns "policy applied" and "result" of the spec are in the detail panel only because the rows of `listAiRequests` carry no policy id; the rule of the open period and the length of the period are enforced by the server only.

### Read-only and aggregate screens (ST-CTR-017, ST-PRJ-011, ST-CAP-012, ST-MNE-008, ST-MNE-010)
- ST-CTR-017 (decision): the six aggregate views CTR-09/11/15/16/17/18 (`apps/web/src/screens/ctr/aggregate/`) take the contract from `?id=`, share one frame (read-only notice, owner links, tab bar) and read only existing owner reads; a guest is detected by the absence of `estimateCeilingVnd` on the contract read and sees no financial column at all (the PAY payment-request view still returns figures to a guest, so the client hides them); a view needs every read permission it uses, else the denied state and no read.
- ST-CTR-017 (gap): the settlement read has no `contract_id` filter (only `project_id`), so the contract is narrowed in the browser; the `contract` parameter on links to QMS-04 is not verified; the contract workspace CTR-03 does not link to the six views; the rule "completion needs no critical NCR" (FR-CTR-15) is shown as a count only, the server owns the rule.
- ST-PRJ-011 (decision): PRJ-08 counts only task-force members whose term names the project (a member without a project follows the whole unit scope of the force and cannot be tied to one project); PRJ-11 reads the handovers of all pages and filters by project in the browser; PRJ-12 shows settled amount, outstanding and unrecovered advance because the read has no "requested" and "approved" value.
- ST-PRJ-011 (gap): `openapi.json` does not list `project_id` on `get_resources_settlement_revision` (the controller accepts it; the screen also filters in the browser).
- ST-CAP-012 (decision): the column "difference" of CAP-12 is the estimate less the registration (the spec does not say of what), blank unless both exist; CAP-13 registers a forecast with the existing `registerDisbursementSchedule` (`kind: FORECAST`), the assumption is required, and project and year live in the URL.
- ST-MNE-008 / ST-MNE-010 (decision): the obligation list carries no `project_id`, so MNE-04 identifies project reports by the `report_kind` of the form and MNE-10 identifies the issued reports by status; the optional reads (names of members) fall back quietly to the short id.
- ST-MNE-010 (gap): no screen exposes `correctMneReport` (F8); `approved_by` is an id, names need `membership.read`; opening a report in MNE-04 reads `getMneReport` twice because the editor mounts after the project card.
- ST-MNE-010 / ST-MNE-008 (gap): the pickers read the calendar (`listMneObligations`), which joins the obligation rules, so a report created without an obligation rule (`MneReportStore.create` allows it) is not listed; the contract has no read that lists the reports of a tenant, so such a report is opened by its id (`?report=`) only.

### Screens on existing operations, batch 2 (ST-ISS-007/008, ST-QMS-002/003, ST-DOC-020, ST-AUD-006, ST-LLM-009, ST-DGV-010)
- ST-ISS-007 (decision): ISS-05 is built on the QMS inspections (`listInspections`, `recordInspectionResult`, `createIssue`) because no ISS checklist backend exists (TK-ISS-007-1 not built); each failed item gets a deterministic `source_ref_id` (UUID from the SHA-256 of inspection id and item code, source module `QMS`) since the database allows one open issue per (source module, source ref); the location goes with the issue only if the inspector ticks consent, and withdrawing it clears the coordinates; a repeated send is `DUPLICATE_SOURCE_OPEN` shown as already existing; if some issues fail the rest are retried alone.
- ST-ISS-008 (decision / gap): ISS-11 never shows title, description, reporter or assignee; source links go to LND-08, PMT-02, QMS-07, PAY-12, CTR-05 and AST-05 only (AUD, DIS, PRJ have none, nor do guest contractors, who get no `source_ref_id`); the issue read has no `source_module` filter so the list is filtered in the browser.
- ST-QMS-002 (decision / gap): QMS-09 keeps the diary in a local queue with its own operation id, drops the queue of another tenant on opening, and shows the `offline` state (not a loading state) when the device is offline and nothing is cached; "block the tenant switch while the queue is not empty" belongs to the shell/session, which has no such hook yet.
- ST-QMS-003 (gap): QMS-10 cannot upload the photo from the web - `registerSitePhoto` needs an `object_key` of kind `site_photo` and no HTTP operation produces one (only the device-bound `post_sync_attachments` flow, with kind `sync_attachment`); the screen takes the reference of an uploaded file, normally filled by the phone app; a web upload operation is needed.
- ST-DOC-020 (gap): DOC-22 signs and returns only (approving drafts stays on DOC-04); DOC-23 "open file" links to DOC-13 because the incoming-document read has no file version id; the 15-minute signing session of the spec is not verified against the server.
- ST-AUD-006 (decision / gap): AUD-09 lists the done actions that have an evidence document (link to DOC-06 by id) - no operation lists the attached evidence, so "attached by" is missing; AUD-11 counts the days with `lateDays` (reminders at 7/3/1 days, unit leader when overdue, second level at 15 days late - the 15 days is the story's assumption, `SECOND_LEVEL_DAYS`) and "notified" means the reminder date has been reached, since no read tells whether a notice was delivered.
- ST-LLM-009 (decision / gap): LLM-11 hashes the typed text in the browser (WebCrypto) and sends only `input_sha256` and `input_ref = playground/<sha>` to `requestAiCompletion`; no operation stores the playground input, the cost estimate is a ceiling the person sets (`max_cost_vnd`), and the input is cleared after each try; LLM-13 needs all of `ai_eval_run.read`, `model_profile_version.read` and `llm.eval.read` and shows the run time as latency; LLM-14 and LLM-15 mask 12-digit identity numbers and Vietnamese telephone numbers in free text; LLM-15 has no guardrail-event stream, so refusals are the requests in `REJECTED_POLICY` or `REJECTED_NO_CITATION` and the controls are the static text of BR-LLM-11.
- ST-DGV-010 (gap): DGV-10 shows completed merges (`listMasterMerges`) because MDM exposes no suspected duplicates or similarity score; DGV-11 shows retention by record class (the catalogue view carries no `retention_policy_id`); DGV-12 treats every master change request as critical (no critical-data flag); DGV-15 shows the counts the server returns for what the caller may see, with the PII flag derived from the PERSONAL classification (no per-dataset row or column policy read).
- ST-IAM-002 (decision / gap): IAM-01/02/03 send the browser to the identity provider (`acr_values=aal2` for the second factor, `login_hint` for the optional account) and never ask for a password, code or email - no login, MFA or recovery operation exists, Keycloak hosts those pages; the locked, expired and failed states are driven by `?reason=` and `?until=` (capped at 15 minutes) and nothing links to them yet (the OIDC callback error path or a session-expiry handler could); `App.tsx` lets these three routes through the signed-out gate; `signIn` takes an optional login hint.
- ST-IAM-008 (gap): IAM-07 uses `searchGlobal`, shows no total, and links only the entity types `project`, `issue` and `conversation` (names assumed - no projection writer exists yet); the header search box (TK-IAM-008-2) belongs to the shell and is not built.
- ST-SAS-021 (gap): SAS-18 is built from `listSystemTenants` (counts by lifecycle, isolation and licence); SLO, incidents, licence lag, outbox age and failed inbox are shown as "no source yet", never as zero, because there is no platform-metrics read.
- ST-OMNI-008 (gap): OMNI-10 is computed in the browser from `get_resources_conversation`; the row has no first-response time, so the rate reads an optional `first_response_at` and says "not computed" until the API sends it; no deadlines in the period is "no basis to compute" (UAT-AC199); response time in working hours, the LLM reply suggestion and contact unmerge have no operation.
- ST-HOME-006 (gap): HOME-05 has no map read model and projects carry no coordinates, so it lists the projects by area as of the chosen date (UAT-AC277) in an accessible table and none on a map (tile hosting and CXN-34 are open); the area comes from N+1 reads capped at 200 projects with a visible notice - a `getHomeMap` read model would remove that.
- ST-LLM-009 (gap, open): the playground of LLM-11 sends `input_ref = playground/<sha256>` for text that is stored nowhere - the gateway (`AiGatewayPort.complete`) expects a pointer to where the requesting module keeps the input, and no operation stores playground input, so an adapter that reads the input cannot resolve it (classification and route rules still run on the request); it needs a protected store for playground input (an operation that takes the text and answers an object key, retained for a short period) before the gateway goes through LiteLLM.
- ST-BID-008 (gap, open): no OCR engine adapter - the pipeline side is `BidOcrStore.recordPages`, which a worker with an engine must call; no preview of the page image beside the recognised text (the document store has no page-render read); BID-05 does not link to BID-06; there is no e2e or visual spec for BID-06 yet.
- ST-BID-008 (gap): BID-06 finds its packages and proposals through the existing reads (`procurement_package.read`, `proposal.read`), so an evaluator who holds only the OCR rights cannot reach the screen; a discovery read under the OCR rights (the proposals with pages waiting) would remove that dependency. The threshold 0.85 is an assumption of the module design until the engine is evaluated in SPIKE-3.
- ST-BID-009 (gap, open): no real model provider or OCR engine (the tests use `MockAiGateway` and `MockEmbeddingPort`); the run is synchronous inside the command (Temporal orchestration is deployment work) and a proposal larger than the profile's input limit is refused with 422 - there is no map-reduce or persistent proposal index; no SCORECARD evidence and no PDF viewer with the box of the quote; the question box of BID-09 is not built (it needs the id of an embedding model the screen cannot discover, so retrieval is covered on the API only); BID-07 takes the AI profile id as text because there is no profile list read; the document set of a run is the one the proposal holds at request time, so a repeat of an older input works only while the set is unchanged; the tables of a page a person corrected are not used for the computed figures until the amounts are corrected in structured form.
- ST-BID-010 (gap, open): the evaluation report and its sign-off through DOC are ST-BID-012 and the award stays `decideProcurementResult`; BID-12 shows the score field on every finding because the finding view carries no `max_score` (the server enforces the scale with 422 `VALIDATION_FAILED`); BID-12 has no "undecided only" filter because the server has none and a client-side one would be wrong across pages; a decision does not write SCORECARD evidence; the comparison takes the latest run that was not cancelled, failed or refused of each proposal and has no ranking or winner by design (BR-BID-09); the unused review columns of `analysis_finding` (V1350) stay in place and are never written.
- ST-BID-011 (gap, open): the letter is only a DOC anchor (`sent_document_id`, a confidential document without a file version) - there is no template for the clarification letter and nothing signs, numbers or distributes it, so the contractor reads the question on BID-11 only; no notification reaches the guest (routing is by unit and project, guests hold contract scopes); no guest upload operation exists, so BID-11 takes the id of a DOC document; the system role GUEST_CONTRACTOR is unchanged (its matrix awaits sign-off), a tenant must define a guest role holding `bid.clarification.read` and `bid.clarification.answer`; no existing screen links to BID-11; the stored status stays SENT after the deadline (readers see EXPIRED, no job writes it).
- ST-BID-012 (gap, open): the minutes template waits for legal confirmation (CXN-BID-03, so the story is DONE_UNCONFIRMED) and each tenant must publish the doc type `BB` before DOC can number the minutes; a report DOC refuses goes back to SIGNED_OFF with all its signatures and the story has no operation to ask DOC again (the runbook sends it to the BID owner); the legal signature of the document is DOC's own signing flow (nothing here fakes it); no minimum team size is enforced; `procurement_result` still takes the minutes as a DOC document id, not an ISSUED report id.
- ST-SAS-018 (gap, open): there is no real data mover or object-storage copy (`MockPlacementMover` keeps a picture of the measurement; production binds an unbound mover that fails every copy with `PLACEMENT_MOVER_UNAVAILABLE`, so no migration can claim to have moved data); the spike on the chosen cloud (SAS-SPK-SILO, D02) is NOT RUN; there is no per-placement connection router (the switch only changes `tenant.placement_id` and `isolation_mode`); creating the silo database and its identity realm belong to provisioning; data written to a silo after the switch cannot be carried back; the flag `sas.silo_promotion` exists but nothing reads release flags for it; no notification is sent for promotion steps; tenant runtime state is cached for up to 1 s per process (a 5 s settle wait covers it, it is not a hard guarantee); the content checksum reads and hashes every row of the tenant on every worker pass and under the freeze, so for a large tenant the freeze window must exceed one measure and the refresh interval should be lengthened.
- ST-SAS-019 (gap, open): there is no real backup or restore engine (`UnboundRestoreEngine` in production answers every request 422 `RECOVERY_POINT_UNAVAILABLE`; `MockRestoreEngine` elsewhere measures the live tenant once and uses it as both backup and copy, so it proves the flow around the engine, not a backup) - it waits on the infrastructure spike and decision D10 (RPO/RTO targets); not built: sandbox cleanup and lifetime, gating `resumeTenant` on a Completed in-place restore, an aal2 step-up on the decision; money totals cover only numeric columns named `*_vnd` and `measure` aggregates whole tables; `evidence_ids` are recorded in the audit row but not validated against documents.
- ST-CTR-016 (gap, open): the adjustment money is on the ledger and in the totals but PAY has no request kind to claim a rise or recover an overpayment on a fall; delay and compensation pricing is not built (the business rule is unspecified; CTR-14 shows schedule impact only); no dry-run preview of the money before the call; two retroactive adjustments on one contract on the same day are refused (422 `NOT_AFTER_REVISION`); a change order can be adjusted only once; a settlement still being worked (SUBMITTED or VERIFYING) does not block the call; a BOQ mismatch is reported before the value-floor check; no Playwright flow or visual-regression region for CTR-14. Decisions (docs/ctr-retroactive-adjustment.md): a new ledger entry kind `ADJUSTMENT` carries the signed difference on the accepted amount only; the difference is worked out per ledger balance against what earlier adjustments left; the retroactive revision is in force from the approval day, not back-dated.
- ST-DOC-021 (gap, open): not connected to a real LGSP - `DOC_INTEROP_TRANSPORT` is bound to `UnconfiguredInteropTransport` (503 `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` to every call, so messages end DEAD with the alert and never claim to be sent), there is no HTTP client for the gateway; the envelope is a JSON stand-in (the real format is edXML, QCVN 102:2016, and the upload pipeline refuses markup, so a real envelope needs its own intake); the inbound `signature` (HMAC-SHA256 over the message fields) was added by this story because the design lists none and a real LGSP's scheme would replace it; retries run as a job (`doc-interop-send`, every minute), not Temporal; a message the receiving agency refuses is REJECTED and not resent; migration V1230 was extended in place (never released) for `last_polled_at`.
- UAT gap work (ST-KERNEL-014/032/033/045/046/052/073/074, IAM-002, CAP-010): `V1461__qms1_quality_acceptance` adds `quality_acceptance.content_hash` NOT NULL with a placeholder default and drops the default, which application N-1 cannot live with and a marker cannot declare in a migration that also adds a column; it is listed by name in `KNOWN_UNMARKED_BREAKS` of the schema compatibility check and QMS must replace the migration; the SLI exporter from the reverse proxy and the budget-report publisher are not built (observability is PLANNED) and the deploy gate fails closed on `feature` releases (`BUDGET_UNKNOWN`) until `SLO_BUDGET_REPORT_URL` is set; LiteLLM's own Langfuse callback must be set to mask or send no text (deployment); no image-building job exists and registry signing with cosign is external; the replication role may need a policy or attribute for the initial snapshot because the tables are FORCE RLS (KERNEL-002 spike); the mobile banner contract (TK-073-3) is not built; no route is behind a feature flag yet (`sas.silo_promotion` and `sas.tenant_restore_in_place` are declared and unbound because those routes act on a target tenant from the path); 25 acceptance criteria are declared external (certificate authority, staging and cloud DR, release pipeline, PowerSync and LiteLLM/Langfuse services, Keycloak, mobile devices).
- Mobile app apps/mobile (ST-MOB-001..041, NAV-02; gap, open): never built or run on a device or an emulator here - `flutter analyze` (0 issues) and `flutter test` (1790) are the only evidence; the Android Gradle/Kotlin and iOS builds, the missing iOS `Podfile`, the sqlite3mc native library on both platforms and `.github/workflows/mobile.yml` have to be proven by the CI runner. Goldens were generated on Windows and may differ on the CI runner. Camera, gallery, location and real EXIF, chunked upload over a mobile network and resume after a kill, TalkBack/VoiceOver, screenshot protection, the one-time code on the leader phone, push delivery and the vendor signing SDK need physical devices (`docs/mobile-device-matrix.md`).
- ST-MOB-007 (gap, open, PARTIAL): only the client and configuration half exists. Missing: the PowerSync adapter, the token endpoint, deployable sync rules, the grant consumer and AC-ST-MOB-007-4 (needs a PowerSync service on a cloud PostgreSQL); offline reads rely on the per-module read operations instead.
- Mobile server contract gaps (open): no operation for tenant discovery, the app version policy (426), 503 maintenance or `DEVICE_REVOKED` yet; the per-tenant `integrity_mode` is not in the contract; the remote-config signature is not checked; the telemetry sink is in memory; no operation hands the phone a `site_photo` object key (QMS-10 keeps the capture but cannot call `registerSitePhoto`, because phone uploads produce `sync_attachment` keys); no endpoint serves the file of an incoming letter (DOC-23 shows the hash and says the file opens on the web); photos from the field cannot be linked to server evidence because no operation accepts a clean `sync_attachment`; `post_reports` is online only; MOB-37 needs an embedding model default server-side; the SYSTEM role GUEST_CONTRACTOR is unchanged (tenants must define a guest role with `bid.clarification.read` and `bid.clarification.answer`); the flag key `omni.external_channels` was chosen by the MOB-26 author.
- Mobile acceptance ids still without a live test (open): AC-ST-MOB-007-4 needs the PowerSync service on a cloud PostgreSQL; 25 further ids are declared external in `docs/story-status.json`.
- Mobile reconnection (gap, open): the queue and upload runners probe a lost connection themselves (15 s, 30 s, 1 min, 2 min, then 5 min, 12 probes, about an hour) because the app has no platform connectivity or app-resume signal; a connectivity plugin (`connectivity_plus`) and an app-lifecycle-resume trigger should call `runner.trigger()` and `reachabilityProvider.report()`, and a phone idle and offline for longer waits for a trigger. The probe policy is `none` in tests and `standard` from `bootstrap.dart`; real timers and a real network are not verified without a device.
- ST-MOB-037 (gap, open): the AI Q&A needs the approved retrieval model (`embedding_model_id` is required by `searchProposalPassages`) but the only way to list models needs `llm.provider.read`, which an evaluator with `bid.evaluate` lacks; the app shows `EMBEDDING_MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` on a 403. Server change needed: make `embedding_model_id` optional so the server uses the tenant's approved retrieval model, or add an operation readable under `bid.evaluate` that returns its id.
- Mobile app, codex round 8 findings accepted as gaps (open, P2): (1) `oidc_client.dart` classifies a token endpoint answer of HTTP 429 as `refreshRejected`, so a rate-limited but valid refresh ends the session and asks for an interactive sign-in; it should be a retryable failure like 5xx. (2) `OidcConfig.parse()` accepts HTTP loopback issuers in debug builds but discovery validates the advertised authorization and token endpoints with the HTTPS-only helper, so a local identity provider advertising HTTP endpoints fails with `discoveryFailed`; the loopback policy has to reach the endpoint validation. (3) `personal_bi/screens/dashboard_screen.dart` filters frozen dataset versions locally on the first page of `listBiDatasetVersions`; with a first page of newer non-frozen versions a LATEST_FROZEN dashboard shows no snapshot, so the request must ask for `status: FROZEN` before pagination and the dataset list must be followed across pages. (4) The same screen chooses the snapshot only in `initState`, so a dashboard version or frozen snapshot that becomes available after a refresh is not picked up, and a refreshed PINNED definition keeps querying its old snapshot; it must be reconciled in `didUpdateWidget`.

- UAT evidence (blocker, 2026-11-04): `npm run uat:gate` is 0/478 screens and 0/28 critical tests because `docs/uat-run-results.json` must be written by a release pipeline that ran the cases against a deployed build with a request id per case and evidence links (docs/runbooks/uat-evidence.md). The web browser lane (541 tests, 812-page route walk) passes but carries UAT ids in only 66 of the 1097 design test cases and records no request ids, so it is not turned into PASS records by hand. Needed: an environment to run the UAT cases (deployed build, per-case request tracing, Android/iOS devices for Maestro, real CA/Keycloak/PowerSync) or the owner's decision on how evidence is recorded.

- UAT critical slice, findings of 2026-11-05 (runner agents, real stack):
  - Design wording to correct: `UAT-NFR-SECURITY-09` (and TX01) name 409 `BALANCE_INSUFFICIENT` for the loser of two simultaneous allocations; CAP.md (`draftCapitalAllocation`, BR-CAP-02) and the error registry say 422 `APPROPRIATION_EXCEEDED` or 409 `VERSION_CONFLICT`, which is what the product does (1,000 rounds: exactly one 200, balance 0, never negative). The case text also says the replay header `Idempotent-Replayed` (the product and the platform docs: `Idempotency-Replayed`), the audit name `report.submitted` (the product: `dis.report_submitted`, event `report.submitted.v1`) and the key `k-001` (under the 16-character minimum).
  - Fixed: a notification whose content is unavailable to the member was marked read when its deep link was opened (UAT-NFR-PRIVACY NOT02); and deep links written as `/screen?id=…` by the jobs never resolved (the resolver took only whole path segments).
  - Open, not a design requirement: the web shell tenant switcher changes tenant without warning about a half-filled form (only the SAS-04 picker asks). The design case TENANCY-07 is the Android PowerSync case (offline queue blocks the change), not this.
  - `UAT-NFR-SECURITY-10`, `TX03`, `NOT01` were not run: the e2e stack starts with `MACHVON_ENVIRONMENT` unset (production), registers no channel adapter and sets no `MACHVON_SECRET_*` webhook key, and does not run the delivery job. Starting it with `MACHVON_ENVIRONMENT=test` and a signing key would make them runnable.
  - `UAT-NFR-TENANCY-03` and `-04` need Redis keys and vector retrieval the stack does not have; `MOB01`, `MOB02` and `TENANCY-07` need the Flutter app on the Android emulator (AVD `guide`, APK not built).

- UAT phone walk, findings of 2026-11-06 (emulator AVD guide, Android 15, debug build, real stack; `scripts/uat-mobile-walk.mjs`):
  - Open: on the home screen (MOB-07) the two disbursement cards print the formula with the names of the fields of the data (`disbursed_treasury_vnd / plan_pm_assigned_vnd * 100`) to the person who reads it; the label should be a sentence in the language of the screen.
  - Open, to confirm on a phone: the back key from the project detail (MOB-10) opened from the site map (MOB-11) went to the home screen (MOB-07) on two walks instead of to the map; the same happens from the account screen (MOB-47) after its queue screen. The walk lost its place there and left that branch.
  - Not a defect of the application: a debug build on software graphics did not show the main content of the home screen inside 2.5 s (3 samples of the cold start flow). The 2.5 s verdict stays NOT RUN until the matrix devices run it; `qa/device/scripts/run_cold_start_samples.sh` read 15000 ms (the timeout) on every sample although the card was on the screen, so it does not read the Flutter labels of this build and cannot be used until it is fixed.
  - Walked: 12 of the 68 screens (MOB-01, 02, 03, 04, 07, 09, 10, 11, 23, 32, 47, 49). The others need seeded data the e2e stack does not hold (a weekly report in draft, approvals, notifications, conversations, contracts) or a device.
  - Second walk (24 screens, `local-2026-11-06-mobile-r2`): the weekly report on the phone has no way in from the navigation of the application. `MOB-13` (choice of the period) is opened only from `MOB-22` (the drafts) and `MOB-22` only from `MOB-13` and from the steps of a draft (`reporting/screens/period_screen.dart`, `drafts_screen.dart`, `widgets/draft_host.dart`); the project rows of `MOB-57` and `MOB-09` do not open it either. A person cannot start a report, so the screens MOB-13 to 22, 55 and 56 are not reachable on the phone (a story gap to confirm: either the entry is missing or it is behind a role that no fixture person has).
  - Not reached, and why: the detail screens that need a record the e2e stack does not seed for the phone person (MOB-24 to 27 approvals and payments, MOB-33 notification, MOB-36 conversation, MOB-37 to 39 AI, MOB-42 conflict, MOB-60 reconciliation, MOB-61 to 68 diaries, quantities, acceptance, handover, signing, land clearance); the screens that need a permission or a sensor of the device (MOB-05, 06, 29, 30); and the state screens (MOB-44 to 46, 52 to 54), which need a server state (session ended, licence expired, maintenance).
